Drupal 12.0 benchmarks

Drupal 12.0.0-beta1, demo_umami profile on Apache+mod_php, nginx+PHP-FPM, the two nginx forks built from source, and FreeUnit, across the database axis.

Overview (307) Drupal 11.4 (18) Drupal 12.0 (70) Symfony (30) WordPress (40) Framework-free PHP (54) ARM64 (Oracle A1) (95)

Everything here is rebuilt from committed results/<run-id>/summary.json files. The null band is printed beside every comparison: it is the same job run with both sides identical (A against a copy of A), so a delta smaller than the band is noise and is reported as no claim. Host class, governor, PHP, Drupal tag, database and runtime versions are in each run's header.

The short version

This page holds 70 published comparisons across 2 application sites, measured on sd-91675, sd-92483. Each comparison is one job: the same cells against two runtimes, with a null phase that bounds the noise first. A CLAIM appears only when the delta is outside that band. Start with the summary tables, then open a run for its numbers; every run links to its raw results/<run-id>/ directory.

Summary: the primary metric per runtime

Primary metric: s instructions (lower is better), measured c32 on one host in one window. The ratio is against the best runtime in that row. Each comparison has its null band printed in the run sections below; a ratio smaller than the band is not a difference. Caveat: this table pools absolute means from runs taken hours apart under a powersave governor, so use it for the order, not for a percentage; the per-run tables are the controlled measurement.
